# Object constexpr C lookup table (index 0)

Candidate `2a876ff` ("Look up object constexprs in a C table before the Python interner") against its parent
`e188d8a`. Motivation: the mix-history journal (`2026-09-28_mix-history_0_6e0d4bc.md`) measured `str` / `tl.dtype`
constexprs alternating at ~800 ns/call against ~105 ns for `int`, because every memo miss called the Python interner.

## Environment

- Candidate `2a876ff` (branch `develop`), clean checkout at `/mnt/nvme2/jinpli/workspace/home/jinpli/development/intj`.
  Parent `e188d8a` as a detached worktree at `/tmp/objlookup-head`. Every raw file records its `commit=`.
- CPU: `bench_mix.py` on `taskset -c 8`, the gate suite on `taskset -c 0`; one process at a time. Shared host:
  other users' pytest jobs pinned to cores 16-183; load averages 32.3 (start) / 25.35, 26.81, 21.66 (gate start) /
  16.38, 17.46, 18.78 (end). Cores 0-15 were not in their affinity masks.
- GPU: AMD Instinct MI308X (`gfx942`), GPU 0 (`HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0`). `bench_mix.py` is host-only (`no_gpu=True`).
- Python 3.12.3 (`/tmp/gb2/bin/python`), Torch `2.14.0+rocm7.2`, Triton `3.8.0`, cc 13.3.0.
- `INTJ_BENCHMARK_ROOT=/tmp/gbench`. CUDA: untested.

## Commands

```bash
# 1. bench_mix.py, parent vs candidate interleaved, 6 processes each (rounds 0-2, then 3-5 to confirm)
#    (driver: /tmp/objlookup-bench/mix.sh; rounds 3-5 ran the same loop by hand)
for r in 0 1 2; do for rev in head cand; do
  cd <tree of rev>; HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 PYTHONPATH=$PWD taskset -c 8 /tmp/gb2/bin/python benchmarks/bench_mix.py \
    > /tmp/objlookup-bench/mix_${rev}_round$r.txt
done; done
python3 /tmp/objlookup-bench/summarize.py     # median of the per-process medians, per row
# 2. the 12-case gate, 3 rounds, core 0
HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 /tmp/intj-bench/run_all.sh obj-lookup
/tmp/gb2/bin/python /tmp/intj-bench/bench_compare.py lazy-task5-final obj-lookup
# 3. flagged GPU rows: ffi_compare and hip_module once more (3 processes), then ffi_compare parent vs candidate
#    interleaved (3 + 3 processes)
PYTHONPATH=$PWD INTJ_BENCHMARK_ROOT=/tmp/gbench HIP_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 taskset -c 0 /tmp/gb2/bin/python \
  benchmarks/bench_ffi_compare.py --iters 1000 --batches 9          # and bench_hip_module_launch.py, same flags
# 4. diagnostic, not committed: intj_object_id_slow marked __attribute__((cold, noinline)), bench_mix 3 + 3 interleaved
```

`bench_mix.py`: 20000 iterations x 2 calls x 9 batches per row, median ns/call.

## Result

**Acceptance: `str` / `tl.dtype` alternating now costs 2-13 ns more than `int` alternating (was ~590-730 ns more).**
Every `str` / `dtype` alternate row dropped by 84-94 % (e.g. 32 args, 50 % str, int others: 815.7 -> 117.3 ns).
At 4 and 16 arguments the gap to the `int` alternate row is 2.2-7.0 ns; at 32 arguments 4.3-13.3 ns, above 10 ns in
three rows (32/25 str tensor 13.3, 32/50 str tensor 10.8, 32/100 str 12.8). Repeat rows (memo hit, unchanged
path) are unchanged except the rows below.

**Rows flagged SLOWER (>3 % and >2 ns, 6 + 6 processes):** 16 args at 50 % share (+2.1-2.2 ns) and 32 args at
50 % share (+3.2-6.0 ns), including rows whose constexprs are all `int` and never reach the object path; also
32/100 str repeat (+3.2). 32 args at 0 % got faster by 2-6 ns in the same runs. The code these rows run did not
change: in `mix_32_50` the parent inlined `intj_object_id_slow` into `intj_call` (0x7f0f bytes), the candidate
calls it out of line (`intj_call` 0x6b67 bytes). A diagnostic build with the slow path marked
`__attribute__((cold, noinline))` removed the 16/50 and 32/50 regressions but moved them to 16/100 str repeat
(+5.9), 32/25 int (+5.4..+6.7) and 32/50-100 str/dtype repeat (+6.1..+11.6), so it was not kept. The history
journal records the same kind of placement shifts at 32 arguments (`a6292eb`, `8d95e66`).

**Gate (12 cases vs `lazy-task5-final`):** every host (ns) row within the rule; `launch_dynamic` str constexpr
`spec_key` 87.3 -> 89.1 ns (+1.8), int constexpr 87.6 -> 89.3 (+1.7), plain 92.3 -> 94.3 (+2.0). Eight GPU (us) rows
flagged: `ffi_compare` INTJ and FFI kernel rows (+10..+22 %) and `hip_module` (+16 %), TVM FFI's own rows included.
Rerun (3 processes): `hip_module` INTJ same function 2.988 us (baseline 2.996); `ffi_compare` rounds 0-1 near
baseline, round 2 slow for FFI and INTJ alike. Parent vs candidate `ffi_compare`, interleaved 3 + 3: INTJ empty
kernel 3.089 vs 3.044 us, INTJ mixed 3.033 vs 3.018, FFI empty 3.387 vs 3.395. So the GPU rows moved with the GPU,
not with this commit.

## Timing caveats

- Shared host with other users' jobs on other cores; the GPU rows drift between runs by 10-20 % (FFI's own rows
  included), so the gate's GPU rows were rechecked by interleaved A/B.
- `bench_mix.py` shares one launcher per (count, share) across its value kinds, so each launcher's kernel cache
  holds several keys; that is identical in both revisions.
- The diagnostic `cold` build was measured uncommitted (source: `2a876ff` plus the one attribute); it is not in
  the tree.

## Raw outputs

Local artifact paths: `/tmp/objlookup-bench/` (`mix_{head,cand}_round<0-5>.txt`, `exp/`), `/tmp/intj-bench/obj-lookup/`
(36 files), `/tmp/intj-bench/obj-lookup-gpurerun/`, `/tmp/intj-bench/obj-lookup-ffi-ab/`.
